- ((b)) As from claim year 2017, to a number of beneficiaries corresponding to that required to cover at least 75 % of the total area determined for the basic payment scheme or the single area payment scheme in the previous year; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-5ec0eae7969bd91cd70f657adb25e90e] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5573b47267e6be3894d4c2a601d16bc]
- ((c)) As from claim year 2018, to all beneficiaries. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5573b47267e6be3894d4c2a601d16bc]

(3.) Where the beneficiary is not in the position to submit the aid application and/or payment claim *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8783425d1ca735bf1b53295ba0045de8] using the geo-spatial aid application form, the competent authority shall provide the beneficiary with either:

- ((a)) the required technical assistance; or
- ((b)) the pre-established forms and the corresponding graphic material in paper. In this case, the competent authority shall transcribe the information received from the beneficiary into the geo-spatial aid application form.

(4.) The pre-established forms provided to the beneficiary shall specify the maximum eligible area per reference parcel in accordance with points (a) and (b) of Article 5(2) of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 640/2014 and the area determined in the previous year per agricultural parcel for the purposes of the basic payment scheme *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ee162d24f43cf9dea0e5df26d6d529aa] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1d48b14c816c17b312fed6ca11f23226] farm sustainability transition payment scheme *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1d48b14c816c17b312fed6ca11f23226] [^c-683ade5d57fdb36698eaf16eec46cb18]... and/or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1d48b14c816c17b312fed6ca11f23226] area-related rural development measure.

The graphic material supplied to the beneficiary in accordance with Article 72(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 shall indicate the boundaries and the unique identification of the reference parcels as referred to in Article 5(1) of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 640/2014 and the boundaries of the agricultural parcels determined in the previous year as to enable the beneficiary to indicate correctly the size and location of each agricultural parcel. As from claim year 2016, it shall also indicate the type, size and location of the ecological focus areas determined in the previous year. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_ac644533-241d-45e8-a411-67898f0db231]

(5. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-b672ad8191904d8e7bb1ada76ad893b2] The beneficiary shall unambiguously identify and declare the area of each agricultural parcel *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-592c5fe758fea93f4716fdbef699d13e] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b672ad8191904d8e7bb1ada76ad893b2] and, where applicable, the type, size and location of the ecological focus areas *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3e563a8319c8b550b21e1c4110358340] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-592c5fe758fea93f4716fdbef699d13e] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b672ad8191904d8e7bb1ada76ad893b2]. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b672ad8191904d8e7bb1ada76ad893b2] With regard to the greening payment, the beneficiary shall also specify the use of the agricultural parcels declared. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-27d2ba18d1719676bb940b3d84badc25] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-592c5fe758fea93f4716fdbef699d13e] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b672ad8191904d8e7bb1ada76ad893b2]

For this purpose, the beneficiary may confirm the information already provided in the pre-established form. However, where the information on the area, location or boundary of the agricultural parcel *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-592c5fe758fea93f4716fdbef699d13e] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b672ad8191904d8e7bb1ada76ad893b2] or, where applicable, the size and location of the ecological focus areas *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1100bac58b7b3fb634169b8557519c77] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-592c5fe758fea93f4716fdbef699d13e] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b672ad8191904d8e7bb1ada76ad893b2] is not correct or is incomplete, the beneficiary shall correct or make changes to the pre-established form. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-592c5fe758fea93f4716fdbef699d13e] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b672ad8191904d8e7bb1ada76ad893b2]

The beneficiary shall unambiguously identify and declare the area of each agricultural parcel. For this purpose, the beneficiary may confirm the information already provided in the pre-established form. However, where the information on the area, location or boundary of the agricultural parcel is not correct or is incomplete, the beneficiary shall correct or make changes to the pre-established form. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b672ad8191904d8e7bb1ada76ad893b2]

(6. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-14b8e7d7ffe1c0541dfbef3747ae75fc] Where the beneficiary is carrying out equivalent practices in accordance with Article 43(3)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 through commitments undertaken in accordance with Article 39(2) of Council Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005 *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-14b8e7d7ffe1c0541dfbef3747ae75fc][^f00014] or Article 28(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013, the commitment shall be indicated in the aid application with reference to the corresponding payment claim. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-14b8e7d7ffe1c0541dfbef3747ae75fc]

For the purpose of regional or collective implementations in accordance with Article 46(5) and 46(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and for the part of the ecological focus area obligations that the beneficiaries have to fulfil individually, beneficiaries participating in such regional or collective implementations shall unambiguously identify and declare, in respect of each agricultural parcel, the type, size and location of the ecological focus area in accordance with paragraph 5 of this Article. - ((a)) all information required for the identification of the parcels sown with hemp, indicating the varieties of seed used; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-e012dc3b6fecb4a171bd7b01629e2a40]
- ((b)) an indication as to the quantities of the seeds used (kg per hectare); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-e012dc3b6fecb4a171bd7b01629e2a40]

By way of derogation from point (c) of the first subparagraph, where sowing takes place after the final date for submitting the single application, the labels shall be submitted by 30 June at the latest. Areas that are not used for the purposes of the aid schemes provided for in *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-e012dc3b6fecb4a171bd7b01629e2a40] Titles III and IV[^c-51b647f561446d52828e6675323c6c39] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4305af10351be2c859088ed858138f51] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-e012dc3b6fecb4a171bd7b01629e2a40]Title III *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-e012dc3b6fecb4a171bd7b01629e2a40] of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 or for the support schemes in the wine sector as referred to in Regulation (EC) No 1308/2013 shall be declared under one or more ‘other uses’ headings. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-e012dc3b6fecb4a171bd7b01629e2a40]

##### Article 18 (affected by repeal, see note). Declaration of a regional or collective implementation[^c-81aaa22d552e8f7d16154a23b983d6de]

For each regional or collective implementation in accordance with Article 46(5) or (6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, a declaration of a regional or collective implementation shall be submitted supplementing the aid application or payment claim of each participating beneficiary. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-81aaa22d552e8f7d16154a23b983d6de]

The declaration shall contain all necessary complementary information to verify compliance with obligations in respect of the regional or collective implementations in accordance with Article 46(5) or (6) of that Regulation, in particular: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-81aaa22d552e8f7d16154a23b983d6de]

- ((a)) the unique identification of each participating beneficiary; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-81aaa22d552e8f7d16154a23b983d6de]
- ((b)) the minimum percentage that each participating beneficiary needs to fulfil individually as referred to in the second subparagraph of Article 46(6) of that Regulation; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-81aaa22d552e8f7d16154a23b983d6de]
- ((c)) the total area of the contiguous structures of adjacent ecological focus areas as referred to in Article 46(5) of that Regulation or of the common ecological focus area as referred to in Article 46(6) of that Regulation, in respect of which obligations are fulfilled collectively; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-81aaa22d552e8f7d16154a23b983d6de]
- ((d)) pre-established graphic material indicating the boundaries and the unique identification of the reference parcels to be used to unambiguously identify the contiguous structures of adjacent ecological focus areas or the common ecological focus area and to indicate their boundaries. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-81aaa22d552e8f7d16154a23b983d6de]

In case of regional implementation, if the detailed plan provided for in Article 46(6) of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 639/2014 contains all the information listed in the second subparagraph of this Article, the declaration referred to in the first subparagraph may be substituted by a reference to the plan. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-81aaa22d552e8f7d16154a23b983d6de]

In case of a collective implementation, the declaration referred to in the first subparagraph shall be supplemented by the written agreement provided for in Article 47(4) of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 639/2014. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-81aaa22d552e8f7d16154a23b983d6de]

#### Section 3 — Other applications

##### Article 20 (affected by repeal, see note). Specific provisions pertaining to aid applications[^c-0234801e0b619e14b576a1a075815942]

(1.)[^c-97ff1f2d4fee318b34fab06078c3ea1a] A beneficiary who does not apply for aid under any of the area-related aid schemes but applies for aid under another scheme listed in Annex 1 to Regulation (EU) 1307/2013 shall, if he has agricultural area at his disposal, declare those areas in his aid application form in accordance with Article 17 of this Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0234801e0b619e14b576a1a075815942]

A beneficiary who is only subject to cross-compliance obligations in accordance with Articles 46 and 47 of Regulation (EU) No 1308/2013 shall declare in his aid application form the areas at his disposal in respect of each calendar year in which those obligations apply. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0234801e0b619e14b576a1a075815942]

[^c-db279346dfc2a5c4297f499c8457f387]. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(2. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ee546305a28ffeb1f7e43015ee284c6a] Every animal keeper shall have the right to obtain from the competent authority without constraint, at reasonable intervals and without excessive delay, information on the data relating to him and his animals kept in the computerised database for animals. ##### Article 23 (affected by repeal, see note). Recovery of undue payment entitlements[^c-88f94a7fdac8095c57e6bb68aa5ebb77]

(1.) Where, after payment entitlements have been allocated to beneficiaries in accordance with Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, it is established that the number of payment entitlements allocated was too high, the number of allocated payment entitlements in excess shall revert to the national reserve or regional reserves referred to in Article 30 of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-88f94a7fdac8095c57e6bb68aa5ebb77]

Where the error referred to in the first subparagraph was made by the competent authority or by another authority and if the error could not reasonably have been detected by the beneficiary, the value of the remaining payment entitlements allocated to that beneficiary shall be adjusted accordingly. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-88f94a7fdac8095c57e6bb68aa5ebb77]

Where the beneficiary concerned by the allocation of a too high number of payment entitlements, meanwhile, has transferred payment entitlements to other beneficiaries, the transferees shall also be bound by the obligation provided for in the first subparagraph in proportion to the number of payment entitlements which have been transferred to them if the beneficiary to whom the payment entitlements had initially been allocated does not have a sufficient number of payment entitlements at his disposal to cover the number of undue payment entitlements. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-88f94a7fdac8095c57e6bb68aa5ebb77]

(2.) Where, after payment entitlements have been allocated to beneficiaries in accordance with Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, it is established that *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-88f94a7fdac8095c57e6bb68aa5ebb77] the payments received by a beneficiary for 2014 as referred to in the first subparagraph of Article 26(2) of that Regulation *(Extent: E)* *(affected by ## TITLE III — CHECKS

### CHAPTER I — Common provisions

#### Article 24. General principles

(1.) Administrative checks and on-the-spot checks provided for in this Regulation shall be made in such a way as to ensure effective verification of:

- ((a)) the correctness and completeness of the information provided in the aid application, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0fb6e33ea8fa8854ffed2327fab65bae] application for support, payment claim or other declaration;
- ((b)) compliance with all eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations for the aid scheme and/or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1e1f9b433393e073ba638e89d5788aa3] support measure concerned, the terms under which aid and/or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-5bd1091c511c320204c0de80a418e202] support or exemption from obligations are granted;
- ((c)) the requirements and standards relevant for cross-compliance. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f2e59163150ff535e958ab58e1eef661]

(2.) The relevant authority[^c-21940a8d8a9637ac35f70ed71ca5d836] shall ensure that compliance with all conditions applicable established by the law applying in the constituent nation[^c-b3d2d4c5911532620c5a634023d7d3c5] and documents containing implementing arrangements or by the rural development programme can be checked according to a set of verifiable indicators to be established by the relevant authority.

(3.) The results of the administrative and on-the-spot checks shall be assessed to establish whether any problems encountered could in general entail a risk for other similar operations, beneficiaries or other bodies. The assessment shall also identify the causes of such situations, any further examination which may be required and necessary corrective and preventive actions.

(4.)[^c-74e293944b7dbbcdc59d4c0152950e69][^c000008] The competent authority shall carry out physical inspections in the field in the event that photo-interpretation of satellite or aerial ortho-images or other relevant evidence including evidence provided by the beneficiary at the request of the competent authority do not provide results that would permit definitive conclusions to be drawn to the satisfaction of the competent authority concerning the eligibility or, where applicable, the correct size of the area that is the subject of administrative or on-the-spot checks.

By way of derogation from the first sub-paragraph, the competent authority may fully substitute the physical inspections to be carried out under this Regulation in respect of claim year 2020 by the use of photo-interpretation of satellite or aerial ortho-images or other relevant evidence including evidence provided by the beneficiary, such as geo-tagged photos, which could permit definitive conclusions to be drawn to the satisfaction of the competent authority. *(Extent: E)*[^c-0588b23ffbad5243fadde922d52d237b]

By way of derogation from the first subparagraph, the competent authority may, fully substitute the physical inspections to be carried out under this Regulation in respect of claim year 2020 by the use of photo-interpretation of satellite or aerial ortho-images or other relevant evidence including evidence provided by the beneficiary, such as geo-tagged photos, which could permit definitive conclusions to be drawn to the satisfaction of the competent authority.[^M_F_f79e8b91-228d-4360-e294-449f02873520]

By way of derogation from the first sub-paragraph, no physical inspection is required in respect of claim year 2020 for on-the-spot checks related to direct payment support and in respect of claim year 2021 for on-the-spot checks related to direct payment support and support for rural development. The competent authority will draw a definitive conclusion from the use of photo-interpretation of satellite or aerial ortho-images or other relevant evidence including such evidence provided by the beneficiary at the request of the competent authority.[^c-471a57a73aa010fdef6b900a8dcaba30]

(5.) This Chapter shall apply to all checks carried out under this Regulation and without prejudice to specific rules provided for in Title IV and V. Paragraph 3 shall however not apply to Title V.

#### Article 25. Announcement of on-the-spot checks[^M_E_bzd4gwli-mnbf-ywck-pdcc-6717c82deb6f9]

*Extent: E*

On-the-spot checks may be announced provided that it does not interfere with their purpose or effectiveness. Any announcement shall be strictly *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0415ac8b3c636fb0e5ef40048e76b625] limited to the minimum time period necessary and shall not exceed 14 days *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c38d4b15ca971f3a33904fa5218b85ea].

However, for on-the-spot checks concerning livestock aid applications or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a562818bd1ef88b4435bafac4ed57e41] payment claims under animal-related support measures or commitments notified in accordance with Article 14a(5), the announcement shall not exceed 48 hours, except in duly justified cases. [^c-919dff8b856b5f9b03446bc08bdb1030]...

#### Article 25. Announcement of on-the-spot checks[^M_E_t8ilznzs-9i6q-fbgj-u4p9-6717c82deb48f]

*Extent: S*

On-the-spot checks may be announced [^c-581973079d448c57fe0b7a526adbc02b].... Any announcement shall be [^c-0405915991aba685eaa2746d601fd3c2]... limited to the minimum time period necessary and shall not exceed 14 days , except in duly justified cases[^c-dff8f300a9aa7c6dc2636ba17d788ec8].

However,[^V_S_42da99dd-b83c-2660-0532-1eee273afd25] [^c-fbf45206e8cfcaaa0a42569ad7b8e75b]... where the legislation applicable to the acts and standards relevant to cross-compliance requires the on-the-spot check to be unannounced, those rules shall also apply in the case of on-the-spot checks related to cross-compliance.

#### Article 25. Announcement of on-the-spot checks[^M_E_2ok2gn8j-62o7-xu94-xvl5-67112e6ab216f]

*Extent: W*

On-the-spot checks may be announced provided that it does not interfere with their purpose or effectiveness. Any announcement shall be strictly limited to the minimum time period necessary [^c-5fef74599e3c598e0fcc2eadf2fd0690]....

However, for on-the-spot checks concerning[^V_W_df20b152-a943-fd82-b5a3-46522dfc44f3] livestock aid applications or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_W_3c828702-a47e-60e8-e048-30f2e63c2f48] payment claims under animal-related support measures or commitments notified in accordance with Article 14a(5), the announcement shall not exceed 48 hours, except in duly justified cases. Furthermore, where the legislation applicable to the acts and standards relevant to cross-compliance requires the on-the-spot check to be unannounced, those rules shall also apply in the case of on-the-spot checks related to cross-compliance.

#### Article 25. Announcement of on-the-spot checks[^M_E_0j9bs682-jil5-e26g-moml-6717c82deb6c0]

*Extent: N.I.*

On-the-spot checks may be announced provided that it does not interfere with their purpose or effectiveness. Any announcement shall be strictly limited to the minimum time period necessary and shall not exceed 14 days.

However, for on-the-spot checks concerning[^V_NI_a5ac3ace-0392-2419-eaa0-366554670e42] livestock aid applications or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_NI_3b94f662-cd01-aa8c-65fe-d0bcce39faca] payment claims under animal-related support measures or commitments notified in accordance with Article 14a(5), the announcement shall not exceed 48 hours, except in duly justified cases. Furthermore, where the legislation applicable to the acts and standards relevant to cross-compliance requires the on-the-spot check to be unannounced, those rules shall also apply in the case of on-the-spot checks related to cross-compliance.

#### Article 26. Timing of on-the-spot checks

(1.) Where appropriate, on-the-spot checks provided for in this Regulation shall be carried out at the same time as any other checks provided for in the law applying in the constituent nation[^c-c225da2b9786fbc26494620b085e8e23].

(2. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4f24cd7a565abbd2ee03d601617a2226] For the purpose of rural development measures in the scope of the integrated system, the on-the-spot checks shall be spread over the year on the basis of an analysis of the risks presented by the different commitments under each measure. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4f24cd7a565abbd2ee03d601617a2226]

The on-the-spot checks on commitments notified in accordance with Article 14a(5) shall be carried out within the time limits that ensure an effective verification of the commitment notified. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4f24cd7a565abbd2ee03d601617a2226]

(3.) On-the-spot checks shall verify compliance with all eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations of those aid schemes or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3ea2202837cabaf442105efb8d30faa1] support measures for which a beneficiary has been selected in accordance with Article 34.

The duration of on-the-spot checks shall be strictly limited to the minimum time period necessary.

(4.)[^c-8465986459eac90545cf820caa41e27b][^c-d5a8c64a0511308652bc3f316170b55f] Where certain eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations can only be checked during a specific time period, the on-the-spot checks may require additional visits at a later date. In such a case, the on-the-spot checks shall be coordinated in such a way to limit the number and the duration of such visits to one beneficiary to the minimum required. Where appropriate, such visits may also be carried out by way of remote sensing in accordance with Article 40. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5a8c64a0511308652bc3f316170b55f]

Where additional visits relating to land laying fallow, field margins, buffer strips, strips of eligible hectares along forest edges, catch crops and/or green cover declared as ecological focus area are required, the number of those additional visits shall for 50 % of the cases concern the same beneficiary, selected on a risk based basis, and for the remaining 50 % of the cases different additionally selected beneficiaries. The different additional beneficiaries shall be selected randomly from all beneficiaries having land laying fallow, field margins, buffer strips, strips of eligible hectares along forest edges, catch crops and/or green cover declared as ecological focus area and such visits may be limited to the areas declared as land laying fallow, field margins, buffer strips, strips of eligible hectares along forest edges, catch crops and/or green cover. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bb9a814f23845670e09e7548316cc674] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5a8c64a0511308652bc3f316170b55f]

Where additional visits are required, Article 25 shall apply to each additional visit. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5a8c64a0511308652bc3f316170b55f]

In respect of claim year 2020, checks under this paragraph may be carried out by the use of new technologies, including geo-tagged photos, or other relevant evidence, as well as remote sensing. *(Extent: E)*[^c-2be28cc7e5eb7e32c3b7f79b00e6040b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5a8c64a0511308652bc3f316170b55f]

In respect of claim year 2020 for on-the-spot checks related to direct payment support and in respect of claim year 2021 for on-the-spot checks related to direct payment support and support for rural development, the requirement for additional visits under this paragraph may be met by the use of new technologies or other relevant evidence.[^c-91d0e0ae34873fae79a49c7362072d84] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5a8c64a0511308652bc3f316170b55f]

#### Article 27. Cross-notification of results of checks[^M_E_ph0503js-hoce-qzhj-y28p-671934dac5aa9]

*Extent: W+S+N.I.*

Where applicable, administrative and on-the-spot checks on eligibility shall take into account suspected cases of non-compliance reported by other services, bodies or organisations.

The relevant authority[^c-c282589459a2cde2cc0f522ad1f8a8b9] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-562c52d0facbd1b12b174321b0d009f6] shall ensure that all relevant findings made in the framework of the checks on compliance with the eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations in respect of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-562c52d0facbd1b12b174321b0d009f6] schemes listed in Annex I to Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-e9d2fe7dc2d119fe47857ae7fdf3317b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-562c52d0facbd1b12b174321b0d009f6], requirements and standards relevant for cross-compliance, and/or support under rural development measures in the scope of the integrated system are cross-notified to the relevant competent authority in charge of granting the corresponding payment. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-562c52d0facbd1b12b174321b0d009f6] The relevant authority[^M_F_b347127b-1810-45a9-fc68-4336408545de] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-6fad044f378abae5aba2b7e4efef5c97] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-44f3ce48bd57186f4c58c90ad0d481d0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-562c52d0facbd1b12b174321b0d009f6] shall also ensure that public or private certification authorities referred to in Article 38 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 639/2014 notify the competent authority in charge of granting the payment for agricultural practices beneficial for the climate and the environment of any finding relevant for the correct granting of such payment to beneficiaries who opted for fulfilling their obligations through the equivalence by certification. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-6fad044f378abae5aba2b7e4efef5c97] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-44f3ce48bd57186f4c58c90ad0d481d0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-562c52d0facbd1b12b174321b0d009f6]

Where the administrative or on-the-spot checks in relation to rural development measures in the scope of the integrated system cover equivalent practices as referred to in Article 43(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, the results of such checks shall be cross-notified for follow-up in respect of granting the payment for agricultural practices beneficial for the climate and the environment. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-44f3ce48bd57186f4c58c90ad0d481d0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-562c52d0facbd1b12b174321b0d009f6]

### CHAPTER II — Administrative checks within the integrated system

#### Article 28. Administrative checks

(1.) The administrative checks referred to in Article 74 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, including cross-checks, shall permit the detection of non-compliances, in particular the automated detection using computerised means. The checks shall cover all elements that are possible and appropriate to control by means of administrative checks. They shall ensure that:

- ((a)) the eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations for the aid scheme or support measure are fulfilled;
- ((b)) there is no double financing through other [^c-bb2e6c6814d2256b3ed397fa12fc5ee4]... schemes;
- ((c)) the aid application or payment claim is complete and submitted within the relevant time-limit and, where applicable, that supporting documents have been submitted and that they prove eligibility;
- ((d)) there is compliance with long-term commitments, where appropriate.

(2.) In respect of animal aid schemes and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0297743a18bf55ea97687a032cae8126] animal-related support measures, a relevant authority[^c-800cf09e34f3441a634720d2923c17df] may, where appropriate, make use of evidence received from other services, bodies or organisations to verify the compliance with the eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations, provided that the service, body or organisation in question is operating to a standard sufficient to control such compliance.

#### Article 29. Cross-checks[^M_E_6z37p5jf-c15c-bh2m-k0y0-671936a90c262]

*Extent: W+S+N.I.*

(1.) Where appropriate, the administrative checks shall include cross-checks:

- ((a)) on declared payment entitlements and on declared agricultural parcels, respectively, in order to avoid multiple granting of the same aid or support in respect of the same calendar or claim year and to prevent any undue accumulation of aid granted under area-related aid schemes listed in Annex I to Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and Annex VI to Council Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-e142464cf9f49f7ce96d218d50bca5d7][^f00017], and area-related support measures as defined in point (21) of the second subparagraph of Article 2 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 640/2014;
- ((b)) on the payment entitlements to verify their existence and the eligibility for aid; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-838fc400c77bd641bcc61cda019a2c61]
- ((c)) between the agricultural parcels as declared in the single application and/or payment claim and the information as contained in the identification system for agricultural parcels per reference parcel in accordance with Article 5(2) of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 640/2014 to verify the eligibility for direct payment scheme and/or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-57d1b62d7ab8fdc85d5c83d699b14417] rural development measure of the areas as such;
- ((d)) between the payment entitlements and the area determined in order to verify that the entitlements are accompanied by at least an equal number of eligible hectares as defined in Article 32(2) to *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-65e0706eebf4b6c50d4dbb08a19ece2b] (6)[^c-aa3c3ba07fae40a3aae5138e772e3aa0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-65e0706eebf4b6c50d4dbb08a19ece2b](4) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-65e0706eebf4b6c50d4dbb08a19ece2b]
- ((e)) by means of the system for the identification and registration of animals, to verify eligibility for the aid and/or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-81331e54bb027f6bd2416d391f06dd4c] support and to avoid undue multiple granting of the same aid and/or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-81331e54bb027f6bd2416d391f06dd4c] support in respect of the same calendar or claim year;
- ((f)) [^c-5826a43e708f44f0eb61382e4ed7495e]. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
- ((g)) [^c-5826a43e708f44f0eb61382e4ed7495e]. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

For the purposes of point (c) of the first subparagraph, where the integrated system provides for geo-spatial aid application forms, the cross-checks shall be carried out as spatial intersection of the digitised area declared with the identification system of agricultural parcels. In addition, cross-checks shall be carried out to prevent double claiming of the same area.

Data from applications or concerning applicants found not to be admissible or not eligible for payment as referred to in Article 34(1) shall be used for the purposes of points (a), (c) and (e) of the first subparagraph of this paragraph.[^c000009]

(2.) Indications of non-compliance resulting from cross-checks shall be followed-up by any other appropriate administrative procedure, and where necessary, by an on-the-spot check.

(3.) Where a reference parcel is subject to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-709116e67cf99a9282b9fdc3068093d0]aaid application and/or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-42e95d003eb94a6a9e18c8abe6931cc1] payment claim by two or more beneficiaries under the same aid scheme or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-45aba63c62dd94f38691393e0e0988e2] under the same support measure and where the agricultural parcels declared are spatially overlapping or where the overall area declared exceeds the maximum eligible area determined in accordance with points (a) and (b) of Article 5(2) of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 640/2014, and the difference falls within the measurement tolerance defined in accordance with Article 38 of this Regulation in respect of that reference parcel, a relevant authority[^c-597b94297f4b263197d2f398a2eb9127] may provide for a proportional reduction of the areas concerned, unless a beneficiary demonstrates that any of the other beneficiaries concerned over-declared his areas to the detriment of the former.

##### Article 30a. Control rates for claim year 2020[^M_E_459665a8-b697-4848-8709-ca87c0e24148][^c-6d2f1cb8300a27c49fa987c8cf098ad9]

*Extent: E+W*

1. By way of derogation from Article 30, the control samples for on-the-spot checks in respect of claim year 2020 must cover at least:

- (a) 1% of all beneficiaries applying for the basic payment scheme;
- (b) 3% of all beneficiaries applying for the payment for young farmers;
- (c) 10% of all the areas declared for the production of hemp.

2. In applying the control rate set out in point (a) of paragraph 1, priority must be given to areas other than those of permanent grassland and/or permanent crops.

##### Article 30a. Control rates for area-related schemes in claim year 2020[^M_E_7f7abe10-6ab2-4006-c504-97253ece896e][^M_F_d360539c-3d0e-46b5-bbfc-68fcbd5dbd36]

*Extent: S*

1. By way of derogation from Article 30, the control samples for the on-the-spot checks in respect of claim year 2020 must cover at least:

- (a) 3% of all beneficiaries applying for the basic payment scheme in accordance with Chapter 1 of Title III of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013;
- (b) 3% of all beneficiaries applying for the payment for young farmers in accordance with Chapter 5 of Title III of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013;
- (c) 3% of all beneficiaries of area-related payments under voluntary coupled support in accordance with Chapter 1 of Title IV of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013.

2. When applying the control rates set out in points (a) and (c) of paragraph 1, priority must be given to areas other than those of permanent grassland and/or permanent crops.

- ((a)) 3%[^c-b5682fee2f366677be52f0c1b841c247] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786] of all beneficiaries required to observe the agricultural practices beneficial for the climate and the environment (hereinafter referred to as ‘the greening practices’), and who are not part of the control populations referred to in points (b) and (c) (hereinafter referred to as ‘the control population for greening’); this sample shall, at the same time, cover at least *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786] 3% of all beneficiaries having areas covered with permanent grasslands which are environmentally sensitive in areas covered by Council Directive 92/43/EEC *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786][^f00018] or Directive 2009/147/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786][^f00019] and further sensitive areas referred to in Article 45(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786]
- ((b)) 3 % of: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786]
  - ((i)) either all beneficiaries qualifying for the greening payment who are exempted from *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786] [^c-9efeed958fc8f30365ca1cc3746de457]... the ecological focus area obligations by not meeting the thresholds referred to in *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786] Article[^c-810f73c7b2913c3204e3bd9798db367a] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786] 46 of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and who are not concerned by the obligations referred to in Article 45 of that Regulation; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786]
  - ((ii)) or, in the years where Article 44 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 639/2014 does not apply *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786] [^V_S_111deb4e-4c05-ea56-0bc9-a69d06849589]..., the beneficiaries qualifying for the greening payment who are exempted from *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786] [^c-42ed6b824beed957cfabfe600f4c4e65]... the ecological focus area obligations by not meeting the thresholds referred to in *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786] Article[^c-be568f60f08c2d1a01e746e5087f0768] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786] 46 of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and who are not concerned by the obligations referred to in Article 45(1) of that Regulation; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786]
- ((c)) 5 % of all beneficiaries required to observe the greening practices and using *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786] [^V_S_b36c1c46-3746-dd86-e005-f4604bf62f94]... environmental certification schemes as referred to in Article 43(3)(b) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786]
- ((d)) 5 % of all beneficiaries participating in *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786] [^V_S_38b93b19-40df-e91f-1391-a8111346c168]... implementation in accordance with Article 46(5) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786]
- ((e)) 5 % of the collective implementation in accordance with Article 46(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786]
- ((f)) 100 % of the contiguous structures of adjacent ecological focus areas as referred to in Article 46(3) of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 639/2014; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786]
- ((g)) 100 % of all parcels on which an obligation to re-convert land into land under permanent grassland according to Article 42 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 639/2014 applies;[^V_S_25e95406-8935-0370-d9cb-829726e39e5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786]
- ((h)) 20 % of all beneficiaries with an obligation to re-convert land into land under permanent grassland according to Article 44(2) and (3) of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 639/2014. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786]

(2.) Beneficiaries who observe the greening practices through equivalent practices in accordance with Article 43(3)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786] [^V_S_18d52966-6f07-89bf-2c93-ed0f7e031388]... or who comply for the whole holding with the requirements laid down in Article 29(1) of Council Regulation (EC) No 834/2007 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786][^f00020] as regards organic farming shall not be part of the control sample and shall not be counted against the control rates laid down in this Article. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786]

(3.)[^V_S_a9a925c3-1817-b4aa-3da6-0b84310f36e5] Where the ecological focus areas are not identified in the identification system for agricultural parcels as referred to in Article 70 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, the control rate laid down in points (a) and (c) to (e) of paragraph 1 shall be supplemented by 5 % of all beneficiaries of the respective control population who are required to have ecological focus area on the agricultural area in accordance with Articles 43 and 46 of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786]

However, the first subparagraph shall not apply where the administration and control system ensures that all ecological focus areas declared are identified and, where applicable, recorded in the identification system for agricultural parcels in accordance with Article 5(2)(c) of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 640/2014 before payment. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_E_vi8o31kg-oto2-knq6-g6su-666c69b2be786]

##### Article 31a. Control rate for the greening payment in claim year 2020[^M_E_4bd9188e-b2e9-40d4-a166-3e25dc95bbcc][^c-09434c11ad89e0e1b673260fbac70439]

*Extent: E+W*

1. By way of derogation from Article 31, the control sample for on-the-spot checks for the greening payment in respect of claim year 2020 is 3% of all beneficiaries required to observe the agricultural practices beneficial for the climate and the environment.

2. The control rate in paragraph 1 must include at least 3% of all beneficiaries having areas covered with permanent grasslands that are environmentally sensitive in areas covered by Council Directive 92/43/EEC or Directive 2009/147/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council and further sensitive areas referred to in Article 45(1) of EUR 2013/1307.

3. In applying the control rate set out in paragraph 1, priority must be given to areas other than those of permanent grassland and/or permanent crops.

##### Article 31a. Control rate for the greening payment in claim year 2020[^c-565b59b044e5e5577d286be23cdd76e1][^M_E_ad3925dd-4b21-4eed-edc6-2c351bfd4a6b]

##### Article 31a. Control rate for the greening payment in claim year 2020[^M_E_8975e8c8-e876-43f2-fe02-d17f92202a67][^M_F_0e38a6d8-3017-491e-9c2a-40e9f861338f]

*Extent: S*

1. By way of derogation from Article 31, the control sample for on-the-spot checks for the greening payment in respect of claim year 2020 is 3% of all beneficiaries required to observe the agricultural practices beneficial for the climate and the environment.

2. The control rate in paragraph 1 must include at least 3% of all beneficiaries having areas covered with permanent grasslands that are environmentally sensitive in areas covered by Council [Directive 92/43/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1992/0043) or [Directive 2009/147/EC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/2009/0147) of the European Parliament and of the Council and further sensitive areas referred to in Article 45(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013.

3. When applying the control rate set out in paragraph 1, priority must be given to areas other than those of permanent grassland and/or permanent crops.

##### Article 32. Control rate for rural development measures[^M_E_wsfg9noq-snwr-o2qs-5kwy-666c73035f7de]

*Extent: S*

(1.) The control sample for on-the-spot checks carried out each year shall cover at least 3%[^c-7af6f6c9e10758332e00016d316d2fe4] of all beneficiaries applying for rural development measures. [^c-aaa871dfcc52b99ca72aa9b11f467ff5]...

[^c-71f1bf0d2560a90adbb48a3e819c8517]...

(2.) By way of derogation from paragraph 1, in the case of groups of persons as referred to in Articles 28 and 29 of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013, each individual member of such groups may be considered as beneficiary for the purpose of calculation of the control rate laid down in paragraph 1.

(2a.)[^V_S_9fa4dff5-23a2-f15a-4ad5-ed85d9f44a3e] By way of derogation from paragraphs 1 and 2, where a relevant authority[^V_S_c3856453-a867-3340-72e6-7dcb919194e9] uses the option to introduce the collective claim, the control sample for on-the-spot checks carried out each year shall cover at least:

- ((a)) 5 % of all collectives submitting a collective claim; this sample shall, at the same time, cover at least 5 % of the total area declared in the collective claim in accordance with Article 14a(3); and
- ((b)) 5 % of the commitments notified in accordance with Article 14a(5).

(3.) For beneficiaries of multi-annual support granted in accordance with Articles 21(1)(a), 28, 29 and 34 of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013 or Article 36(a)(iv) and (v), (b)(i), (iii) and (v) of Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005 involving payments exceeding five years, the relevant authority[^V_S_4e20d30e-b01e-2fe0-7290-b9a501c02cd3] may decide, after the fifth year of payment, to check at least 2,5 % of those beneficiaries.

The first subparagraph shall apply to support granted under the third subparagraph of Article 28(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013 after the fifth year of the payment for the relevant commitment.

Where a relevant authority[^V_S_a824c8cc-ea24-0b68-9dda-161233e37fab] uses the option to introduce the collective claim, this paragraph shall not apply to collectives.

(4.) Beneficiaries checked under paragraph 3 shall not be taken into account for the purpose of paragraph 1.

For the purposes of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-5e19aab6868c1bb67095b05a4815bb7a] [^c-63b58653f1ed3a39909196de56fda544]... *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-5e19aab6868c1bb67095b05a4815bb7a] point (c) of Article 40a(1), the same beneficiary may be used to respect several of the minimum control rates concerned provided that the effectiveness of selection of the risk based samples required therein is not affected. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-5e19aab6868c1bb67095b05a4815bb7a]

(2.)[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa] For the purposes of Articles 30 and 31, the sample selection shall ensure that: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa]

- ((a)) between 20% and 25% of the minimum number of beneficiaries subject to on-the-spot checks in accordance with[^c-8a1720120282266df65e0cc6dacaf42b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa] points (a) to (f) and (h) of Article 30 and in *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa] points (a) to (e)[^V_S_aaaf5fe8-edae-ab5b-351b-636091a10dc4] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa] of Article 31(1) shall be selected randomly; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa]
- ((b)) between 20% and 25% of the minimum number of beneficiaries subject to on-the-spot checks in accordance with[^c-6181c4bc691627625baa1fef17e5a184] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa] point (b) of Article 31(1) shall be selected randomly; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa]
- ((c)) between 4 % and 5 % of the control population referred to in point (h) of Article 31(1) shall be selected randomly; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa]
- ((d)) the remaining number of beneficiaries in the control sample referred to in points (a) to (e) and (h) of Article 31(1) shall be selected on the basis of a risk analysis. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa]

For the purposes of Article 31, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa] the relevant authority[^V_S_527be8c4-81da-c051-cd4d-3c62b910dbb8] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa] shall ensure representativeness of the control sample as regards the different practices. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa]

The additional beneficiaries to be subject to on-the-spot checks for the purposes of the first subparagraph of Article 31(3) shall be selected on the basis of a risk analysis. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa]

Where the competent authority decides to apply the option provided for in Article 40a(4) or in Article 70a(3), findings revealed by checks by monitoring in the previous claim year shall be taken into account in the risk analysis referred to in point (d) of the second subparagraph.[^V_S_99a3904f-d92f-dc07-c9ed-3b93283e46a0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_fdeefae1-6906-e703-39ae-8c9aaf4327aa]

The remaining number of beneficiaries and commitments to be subject to on-the-spot checks shall be selected on the basis of a risk analysis. Where the competent authority decides to apply the option provided for in Article 40a(4) or in Article 70a(3), findings revealed by checks by monitoring in the previous claim year shall be taken into account in the risk analysis. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_143dff85-7f35-ba2f-9821-29f287fa79f2]

For the purposes of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_143dff85-7f35-ba2f-9821-29f287fa79f2] Article *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_143dff85-7f35-ba2f-9821-29f287fa79f2] 32 [^V_S_0c689a50-fca9-5910-0477-93b069c7e641]..., the random part of the sample may also include either the beneficiaries already randomly selected in accordance with points (a), (b) and (c) of paragraph 2 or the additional beneficiaries selected randomly in accordance with the second subparagraph of Article 26(4) or both. For the purposes of Article 32, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_143dff85-7f35-ba2f-9821-29f287fa79f2] the relevant authority[^V_S_cba9ef32-5a6f-fa9a-cf0b-41bd612c2feb] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_143dff85-7f35-ba2f-9821-29f287fa79f2] may, as a result of the risk analysis, select specific rural development measures which apply to the beneficiaries. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_143dff85-7f35-ba2f-9821-29f287fa79f2]

(3.) For the purposes of Article 33, between 20% and 25% of the minimum number of beneficiaries to be subject to on-the-spot checks shall be selected randomly. The remaining number of beneficiaries to be subject to on-the-spot checks shall be selected on the basis of a risk analysis.

For the purposes of Article 33, the random part of the sample may also include either the beneficiaries already randomly selected in accordance with points (a), (b) and (c) of paragraph 2 or the additional beneficiaries selected randomly in accordance with the second subparagraph of Article 26(4) or both. The number of such beneficiaries in the control sample shall not go beyond their proportion in the control population.

(5.) The effectiveness of the risk analysis shall be assessed and updated on an annual basis as follows:

- ((a)) by establishing the relevance of each risk factor;
- ((b)) by comparing the results as regards the difference between the area declared and the area determined of the risk based and randomly selected sample; or by comparing the results as regards the difference between the animals declared and the animals determined of the risk based and randomly selected sample;
- ((c)) by taking into account the specific situation and, where appropriate, evolution of the risk factors’ relevance in the constituent nation[^V_S_3429d015-6ecf-f487-ccd9-225028e714dc] relevant territory[^V_S_026f79b3-ccfc-6702-eeee-7f748e17dc6d];

(7.) Where appropriate, a partial selection of the control sample may be made on the basis of available information before the final date referred to in Article 13. That provisional sample shall be completed when all relevant aid applications or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^V_S_ffce1a61-aa9a-5296-20e6-09900bf6f246] payment claims are available.

##### Article 34. Selection of the control sample[^M_E_044fcd8a-5527-4471-c8e8-8a0251c61b54][^c-127fa9475bf3dc026960ddd320a5ec36]

*Extent: N.I.*

The control samples established in article 30 may be selected randomly or on the basis of a risk analysis.

##### Article 35 (affected by repeal, see note). Increase of the control rate[^c-0804a7cd76d9a85bfd15ee4d2dfcb472]

Where on-the-spot checks reveal any significant non-compliance in the context of a given *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0804a7cd76d9a85bfd15ee4d2dfcb472] aid scheme or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1d1ffc878837e2c57513243265306305] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0804a7cd76d9a85bfd15ee4d2dfcb472] support measure *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0804a7cd76d9a85bfd15ee4d2dfcb472] or in a region or part of a region *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-6d9973a3ccaf20d8c9317bbcc51fb103] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0804a7cd76d9a85bfd15ee4d2dfcb472], the competent authority shall appropriately increase the percentage of beneficiaries to be checked on-the-spot in the following year. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0804a7cd76d9a85bfd15ee4d2dfcb472]

##### Article 36 (affected by repeal, see note). Reduction of the control rate[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d]

(1.) The control rates laid down in this Chapter may only be reduced in respect of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d] aid schemes or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7c55e31840b4437e99521bddcdc40e52] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d] support measures set out in this Article. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d]

(2.)[^c-39efef26b3da0c96f9b4a00fb6371fb4] By way of derogation from *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ba2109ba9dce96a2f735534d9aecce2f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d] Article 30(a)[^c-0f32be40868d0a0b989df7100d54bc6d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ba2109ba9dce96a2f735534d9aecce2f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d] and (b) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b3556381f75d1cbf5e257b0233e62f38] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ba2109ba9dce96a2f735534d9aecce2f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d], the relevant authority[^c-5f19393b1dd5ef8d5b7d79d4c716f9f7] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ba2109ba9dce96a2f735534d9aecce2f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d] may, as regards the basic payment scheme *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ba2109ba9dce96a2f735534d9aecce2f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d] and[^c-f9f6b2318a031ef558cd3ae9b2dba927] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7476e9ab7c042b41e82e757d33b3de62] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ba2109ba9dce96a2f735534d9aecce2f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d] the re-distributive payment *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7476e9ab7c042b41e82e757d33b3de62] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ba2109ba9dce96a2f735534d9aecce2f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d][^c-dac80a41ba3281344edb5c0727eaef6a]..., decide to reduce the minimum level of on-the-spot checks carried out each year per scheme to *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ba2109ba9dce96a2f735534d9aecce2f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d] 1%[^c-64dcbc0f1ce16e9c9a3867bc77201d9e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ba2109ba9dce96a2f735534d9aecce2f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d].

The first subparagraph shall only apply if a system of spatial intersection of all aid applications with the identification system for agricultural parcels is in place *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ba2109ba9dce96a2f735534d9aecce2f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d] [^c-9ae257acf365ee6bc02d74eabc5ef96e]....

In respect of claim year 2015 the rate of errors found in the random sample checked on the spot shall not exceed 2 % in the preceding two financial years. That rate of errors shall be certified by the Member State in accordance with the established methodology drawn up at Union level. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ba2109ba9dce96a2f735534d9aecce2f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20e787beabdca43d7743b6242876430d]

In respect of claim year 2016 the rate of errors found in the random sample checked on the spot shall not exceed 2 % in the preceding financial year.
