##### Article 38. Area measurement

(1.) The actual area measurement of the agricultural parcel as part of an on-the-spot check may be limited to a randomly selected sample of at least 50 % of the agricultural parcels for which an aid application or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-849fd9e4ea46b37984def6b44b435343]a payment claim has been submitted under the area-related aid schemes or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a27769c1c32618ebb7554945b4934700] rural development measures. When this sample check reveals any non-compliance, all agricultural parcels shall be measured or conclusions from the sample shall be extrapolated.

(2.) Agricultural parcel areas shall be measured by any means proven to assure measurement of quality at least equivalent to that required by applicable technical standards, as drawn up in the relevant constituent nation[^c-d7be46ac53af67d695d3862a15f0f770].

(3.) The competent authority may make use of remote sensing in accordance with Article 40 and Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) techniques where possible.

(4.) A single value buffer tolerance shall be defined for all area measurements performed using GNSS and/or ortho-imagery. For this purpose the measurement tools used shall be validated for at least one validation class of buffer tolerance below the single value. However, the single tolerance value shall not exceed 1.25 m.

The maximum tolerance with regard to each agricultural parcel shall not, in absolute terms, exceed 1.0 ha.

However, for the measures referred to in Article 21(1)(a) and Articles 30 and 34 of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013 as far as the forestry area is concerned, the relevant authority[^c-a88c510321d6d2fbeefdab7bbabffeef] may define appropriate tolerances, which shall in no case be greater than twice the tolerance set out in the first subparagraph of this paragraph.

(5.) The total area of an agricultural parcel may be taken into account in the measurement provided that it is fully eligible. In other cases the net eligible area shall be taken into account. For that purpose, the pro-rata system as referred to in Article 10 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 640/2014 may be applied, where appropriate. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7c7b612d6553d7bf194eed1eef9bd525]

(6.)[^c-9f56435a5bdb92683d98a7159f370ee3] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(7.) Where Article 17 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 640/2014 may lead to an artificial division of the area of adjacent agricultural parcels with a homogeneous land cover type into separate agricultural parcels, the measurement of this area of adjacent agricultural parcels with a homogeneous land cover type shall be combined in one single measurement of the agricultural parcels concerned.

(8. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-1bdc93baeb4e0e6faaf0447d2d1024e2] Where appropriate, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1bdc93baeb4e0e6faaf0447d2d1024e2] separate measurements *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1bdc93baeb4e0e6faaf0447d2d1024e2] shall be carried out on the agricultural parcel for the purpose of the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1bdc93baeb4e0e6faaf0447d2d1024e2] basic payment scheme[^c-dfbb0faaf8dcc187d512fc4bca9336f3] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1bdc93baeb4e0e6faaf0447d2d1024e2]farm sustainability transition payment scheme *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1bdc93baeb4e0e6faaf0447d2d1024e2][^c-09f329f1d39213d5d7e4f746a2b65251]... in accordance with Chapter 1 of Title III of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and a spatially different overlapping agricultural parcel for the purpose of the remaining area-related aid schemes and/or rural development measures, where appropriate. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1bdc93baeb4e0e6faaf0447d2d1024e2]

(9.)[^c-7b681b27c9892367250e5ec27b9e3075][^c000011] Where the eligible area measured in accordance with *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b681b27c9892367250e5ec27b9e3075] paragraphs 1 to 8 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9981ac1db3b630380d5a4d2d328c8327] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b681b27c9892367250e5ec27b9e3075]this Article *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b681b27c9892367250e5ec27b9e3075] is different from the area established as a basis for the calculation of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b681b27c9892367250e5ec27b9e3075] aid or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-20b8f8ce5db6830528611834881cd599] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b681b27c9892367250e5ec27b9e3075] support where checks by monitoring are applied in accordance with Article 40a, the area measured in accordance with *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b681b27c9892367250e5ec27b9e3075] paragraphs 1 to 8 of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-260e55f2144ee69259bb97e6dcee698a] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b681b27c9892367250e5ec27b9e3075] this Article shall prevail. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b681b27c9892367250e5ec27b9e3075]

(10.) In the specific case of agricultural parcels of permanent grassland or permanent pastures *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-abbcbd6c387422e3a7ac79b420df8acf] used in common by several beneficiaries, the actual measurement may be substituted by checks based on the ortho-images used for the update of the identification system for agricultural parcels referred to in Article 70 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 provided that those checks are carried out on all such parcels within a period of maximum three years and the competent authority can demonstrate effective operational procedures complying with the rules laid down in Article 7 of this Regulation and effects the recoveries appropriately.

##### Article 39. Verification of eligibility conditions

(1.) The verification of the eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations of the agricultural parcels as part of an on-the-spot check may be limited to a randomly selected sample of at least 50 % of the agricultural parcels for which an aid application or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b9acb1e1262743a0350976ba4b29bc0b]a payment claim has been submitted under the area-related aid schemes or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ee2f67807ec957231d110579f3fc298c] rural development measures.

However, for rural development measures, when some eligibility criteria, commitments or other obligations of the agricultural parcels cannot be adequately checked when limiting the checks to a randomly selected sample in accordance with the first subparagraph, an additional risk based sample allowing the checking of these criteria, commitments or obligations shall be selected.

When the randomly selected sample or the risk based sample reveal any non-compliance, all agricultural parcels shall be subject to the verification of the eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations or conclusions from the sample shall be extrapolated.

The eligibility of agricultural parcels shall be verified by any appropriate means, including evidence provided by the beneficiary at the request of the competent authority. That verification shall also include a verification of the crop, where appropriate. To that end, additional proof shall be requested where necessary.

(2.)[^c-1d50633dc9fb75319a833b5461549289] For permanent grassland which can be grazed and which forms part of the established local practices where grasses and other herbaceous forage are traditionally not predominant in grazing areas, the reduction coefficient according to Article 32(5) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 may be applied, where appropriate, to the eligible area measured in accordance with Article 38 of this Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-53f5409ac6c6cbdb081f4a3dfb8a68fb] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1d50633dc9fb75319a833b5461549289] Where an area is used in common, the competent authorities shall allocate it between the individual beneficiaries in proportion to their use or right of use of it. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1d50633dc9fb75319a833b5461549289]

(3.) Landscape features declared by beneficiaries as ecological focus area that are not included in the eligible area in accordance with Articles 9 and 10 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 640/2014 shall be verified according to the same principles as applicable to the eligible area. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0f28b1c7b1f3b805d2d95a911f15bbd0]

(4. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-09c1f4953eaa89e2423fb2d33d3cec13] In the specific case of agricultural parcels of permanent grassland or permanent pastures used in common by several beneficiaries, the verification of the eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations may be substituted by checks based on the ortho-images used for the update of the identification system for agricultural parcels referred to in Article 70 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 provided that those checks are carried out on all such parcels within a period of maximum three years and the competent authority can demonstrate effective operational procedures complying with the rules laid down in Article 7 of this Regulation and effects the recoveries appropriately. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-09c1f4953eaa89e2423fb2d33d3cec13]

##### Article 40. Checks with remote sensing

Where a relevant authority[^c-16b7911222d75381e2019db1edd777c0] carries out on-the-spot checks by remote sensing, the competent authority shall:

- ((a)) perform photo interpretation of ortho-images (satellite or aerial) of all agricultural parcels per aid application and/or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-6243b1715ccecdb01c65c2f94f51e437] payment claim to be checked with a view to recognising the land cover types, and where appropriate the crop type, and measuring the area;
- ((b)) carry out physical inspections in the field of all agricultural parcels for which photo interpretation or other relevant evidence requested by the competent authority does not make it possible to verify the accuracy of the declaration of areas to the satisfaction of the competent authority;
- ((c)) carry out all checks required to verify the compliance with the eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations of the agricultural parcels;
- ((d)) take alternative action to cover the area measurement in accordance with Article 38(1) of any parcels not covered by imagery.

##### Article 40a (affected by repeal, see note). Checks by monitoring[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]

(1.) Competent authorities may carry out checks by monitoring. Where they elect to do so, they shall: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]

- ((a)) set up a procedure of regular and systematic observation, tracking and assessment of all eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations which can be monitored by Copernicus Sentinels satellite data or other data with at least equivalent value, over a period of time that allows to conclude on the eligibility of the aid or support requested; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]
- ((b)) carry out, where necessary, and in order to conclude on the eligibility of aid or support requested, appropriate follow-up activities; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]
- ((c)) carry out checks for 5 % of the beneficiaries concerned by eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations which cannot be monitored by Copernicus Sentinels satellite data or other data with at least equivalent value, and are relevant to conclude on the eligibility of aid or support. Between 1 % and 1,25 % of the beneficiaries shall be selected randomly. The remaining beneficiaries shall be selected on the basis of a risk analysis; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]
- ((d)) inform beneficiaries about the decision to carry out checks by monitoring and set up appropriate tools to communicate with beneficiaries on at least provisional results at parcel level of the procedure set up in accordance with point (a) of this subparagraph, warning alerts and evidence requested for the purposes of points (b) and (c). Competent authorities shall ensure timely communication with the beneficiaries to support them in fulfilling the eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations and allow the beneficiaries to address or remedy the situation before the conclusions are drawn in the control report referred to in Article 41. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]

For the purposes of points (b) and (c) of the first subparagraph, physical inspections in the field shall be carried out when relevant evidence, including evidence provided by the beneficiary at the request of the competent authority, does not allow to conclude on the eligibility of the aid or support requested. Physical inspections in the field may be limited to checks of eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations that are relevant to conclude on the eligibility of the aid or support requested. These physical inspections shall include area measurement only where necessary to conclude on the compliance with these eligibility criteria, commitments or other obligations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]

For the purposes of point (c) of the first subparagraph, checks of eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations which cannot be monitored by Copernicus Sentinels satellite data or other data with at least equivalent value may be limited to a sample of at least 50 % of the agricultural parcels declared by a beneficiary. The competent authority may select this sample randomly or on the basis of other criteria. Where the sample of agricultural parcels is selected randomly and the checks reveal any non-compliance, the competent authority shall extrapolate the conclusions from the sample or shall check all agricultural parcels. Where the sample is selected on the basis of other criteria and the checks reveal any non-compliance, the competent authority shall check all agricultural parcels. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]

(2.) Where the competent authority carries out checks by monitoring in accordance with paragraph 1, can demonstrate effective operational procedures fulfilling the requirements laid down in Articles 7, 17 and 29 of this Regulation and has proven the quality of the identification system for agricultural parcels as assessed in accordance with Article 6 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 640/2014: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]

- ((a)) Articles 25, 26, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] 30, 31,[^c-9ca0ad8e3d2fbcbfd898d8e462f370f3] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] 32, 34, 35, 36, Article 37(2), (3) and (4) and Articles 38 and 40 of this Regulation do not apply; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]
- ((b)) the verification of tetrahydrocannabinol content in hemp growth carried out in accordance with Article 9 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 639/2014 shall be made for 30 % of the area or 20 % of the area in case the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-2eeed4231308fdfe748e59a150b5ed39] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] relevant authority[^c-39e9c7ab044af45836afbab62c3f7c4a] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-2eeed4231308fdfe748e59a150b5ed39] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] has a system of prior approval in place. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-2eeed4231308fdfe748e59a150b5ed39] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]

(3.) The competent authority may decide to apply checks by monitoring at the level of the individual *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] area-related aid scheme or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-cb5337346a8148554d154821e6f3ad9c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] support measure or type of operation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] or to defined groups of beneficiaries subject to on-the-spot checks for the greening payment, as referred to in points (a) to (h) of Article 31(1) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-11110441c255ff2b9aac77d24303f6fb] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5].

In the first two years of application, the competent authority may decide to apply checks by monitoring to beneficiaries of an aid scheme or support measure in areas chosen on the basis of objective and non-discriminatory criteria. In such cases the areas subject to checks by monitoring in the second year of application shall be greater than in the first year of application. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]

Where the competent authority decides to carry out checks in accordance with the first or second subparagraph, paragraphs 1 and 2 shall apply only to the beneficiaries subject to checks by monitoring. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]

(4.) Where the procedure referred to in point (a) of paragraph 1 reveals findings relevant for *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] direct payment schemes, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-81e05ca3edc3643e5726d08f81a35f1d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] rural development measures and requirements and/or standards not checked by monitoring, competent authorities may decide to take into account those findings only in respect of beneficiaries selected in accordance with Articles *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] 30, 31,[^c-3a29e15473d80ead8181cb593228de63] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] 32 and 68 for on-the-spot checks of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] direct payment schemes, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-81e05ca3edc3643e5726d08f81a35f1d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] rural development measures and requirements and/or standards not checked by monitoring. The derogation shall be limited to the three years following the 1 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]<sup> st *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5] </sup> of January of the calendar year in which the competent authority started carrying out checks by monitoring. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22c67fe85310e5fef9d49fc09301fcb5]

##### Article 40b. Notifications[^c-a3ad6575f2aa8e2b7b65e2764bcf66ec]

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

##### Article 41. Control report

(1.) Every on-the-spot check under this Section shall be the subject of a control report which makes it possible to review the details of the checks carried out and to draw conclusions on the compliance with the eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations. The report shall indicate in particular:

- ((a)) the aid schemes or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f28beee724c142b9e40e277dcd17ba5a] support measures , the aid applications *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-61ddcfab0667effd0116186a008b500b] or payment claims checked;
- ((b)) the persons present;
- ((c)) the agricultural parcels checked, the agricultural parcels measured including, where applicable, the result of the measurements per measured agricultural parcel and the measuring methods used;
- ((d)) where applicable, the results of the measurement of non-agricultural land for which support under rural development measures is being claimed and the measuring methods used;
- ((e)) whether notice of the check was given to the beneficiary and, if so, the period of prior notice;
- ((f)) indications of any specific control measures to be carried out in the context of individual aid schemes or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f28beee724c142b9e40e277dcd17ba5a] support schemes;
- ((g)) indication of any further control measures carried out;
- ((h)) indication of any non-compliance found that could require cross-notification in view of other aid schemes, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-cc0bbe9d3f10491d1d484e521f395b15] support measures and/or cross-compliance *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-322bf029872ee46df7c3c70a13ab1b93];
- ((i)) indication of any non-compliance found that could require follow-up during the following years.

Where checks by monitoring are carried out in accordance with Article 40a, points (b) to (e) of the first subparagraph shall not apply. The control report shall indicate the results of the checks by monitoring at parcel level. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0543683e003f62dfe644eb60e127e29d]

(2.) The beneficiary shall be given the opportunity to sign the report during the check to attest his presence at the check and to add observations. Where the relevant authority makes[^c-790b3afd19719300080694d22e8c0c7f] use of a control report established by electronic means during the check, the competent authority shall provide for the possibility of an electronic signature by the beneficiary or the control report shall be sent without delay to the beneficiary giving him the opportunity to sign the report and to add any observations. Where any non-compliance is found the beneficiary shall receive a copy of the control report.

By way of derogation from the first subparagraph, where the relevant authority[^c-4ad85b49c46086b266be1a7b3d099f25] uses the option to introduce collective claims, the relevant authority may decide not to give the collective the opportunity to sign the control report if no non-compliance is revealed during the check. If any non-compliance is revealed as a consequence of such checks, the opportunity to sign the report shall be given before the competent authority draws its conclusions from the findings with regard to any resulting reductions, refusals, withdrawals or penalties.

Where the on-the-spot check is carried out by means of remote sensing in accordance with Article 40 or by means of monitoring in accordance with Article 40a, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ed7a10ca6a51bf34b80182cfc41853ed] the relevant authority[^c-b3cb99d479738c981b5a86a644fad852] may decide not to give the beneficiary the opportunity to sign the control report if no non-compliance is revealed during the check by remote-sensing or by monitoring *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4d3142d2ce652d1a96021834e9375f2b]. If any non-compliance is revealed as a consequence of such checks or by monitoring *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4d3142d2ce652d1a96021834e9375f2b], the opportunity to sign the report shall be given before the competent authority draws its conclusions from the findings with regard to any resulting reductions, refusals, withdrawals or administrative penalties. Where checks by monitoring are applied, this obligation shall be considered fulfilled if beneficiaries are notified of non-compliances through the tools set up to communicate with beneficiaries in accordance with point (d) of Article 40a(1) and beneficiaries are given the opportunity to contest the non-compliances before the competent authority draws its conclusions from the findings with regard to any resulting reductions, refusals, withdrawals or administrative penalties. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1eeda6f5aa91003ec7c71769969d42e5]

E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-307b459e3697b90b86d729acad04ace0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4b00e6a5697434a62c991d3a9335ce9b]may *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-307b459e3697b90b86d729acad04ace0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4b00e6a5697434a62c991d3a9335ce9b] be spread over the period in which an animal may qualify for the payment or support. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-307b459e3697b90b86d729acad04ace0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4b00e6a5697434a62c991d3a9335ce9b]

Where the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4b00e6a5697434a62c991d3a9335ce9b] relevant authority[^c-b09cdaac0350c9b4003dda4e2b6138de] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4b00e6a5697434a62c991d3a9335ce9b] makes use of the possibility provided for in Article 21(3), the potentially eligible animals as defined in point (17) of the second subparagraph of Article 2(1) of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 640/2014 shall also be checked. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4b00e6a5697434a62c991d3a9335ce9b]

On-the-spot checks shall include in particular a check that the number of animals present on the holding for which *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4b00e6a5697434a62c991d3a9335ce9b] aid applications and/or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7e4dddaccaefbad7f792150815915e33] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4b00e6a5697434a62c991d3a9335ce9b] payment claims have been submitted and, where applicable, the number of potentially eligible animals corresponds to the number of animals entered in the registers and to the number of animals notified to the computerised database for animals. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4b00e6a5697434a62c991d3a9335ce9b]

(2.) On-the-spot checks shall also include checks: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4b00e6a5697434a62c991d3a9335ce9b]

- ((a)) of the correctness and coherence of entries in the register and the notifications to the computerised database for animals on the basis of a sample of supporting documents such as purchase and sales invoices, slaughter certificates, veterinary certificates and, where applicable, animal passports or movement documents, in relation to animals for which aid applications or payment claims were submitted in the six months prior to the on-the-spot check; however if anomalies are found, the check shall be extended to 12 months prior to the on-the-spot check; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4b00e6a5697434a62c991d3a9335ce9b]
- ((b)) that bovine or ovine/caprine animals are identified by ear tags or other means of identification, accompanied, where applicable, by animal passports or movement documents and that they are recorded in the register and have been duly notified to the computerised database for animals. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4b00e6a5697434a62c991d3a9335ce9b]

The checks referred to in point (b) of the first subparagraph may be made on the basis of a random sample. When this sample check reveals any non-compliance, all animals shall be checked, or conclusions from the sample shall be extrapolated. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4b00e6a5697434a62c991d3a9335ce9b]

##### Article 43 (affected by repeal, see note). Control report in relation to animal aid schemes and animal-related support measures[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d]

(1.) Every on-the-spot check under this Section shall be the subject of a control report which makes it possible to review the details of the checks carried out. The report shall indicate in particular: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d]

- ((a)) the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d] animal aid schemes and/or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-391115377417e580924d37a32e7704fc] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d] animal-related support measures and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d] livestock aid applications and/or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-6c82d4c77e2dfdb6bdc9fd7eaf279b15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d] payment claims checked; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d]
- ((b)) the persons present; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d]
- ((c)) the number and type of animals found and, where applicable, the ear tag numbers, entries in the register and in the computerised databases for animals and any supporting documents checked, the results of the checks and, where applicable, particular observations in respect of individual animals and/or their identification code; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d]
- ((d)) whether notice was given to the beneficiary of the visit and, if so, the period of prior notice. Particularly where the limit of 48 hours referred to in Article 25 is exceeded the reason shall be stated in the control report; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d]
- ((e)) indications of any specific control measures to be carried out in the context of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d] animal aid schemes and/or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-11e8295fe6eec0bf467f3c28bf79c18e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d] animal-related support measures; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d]
- ((f)) indication of any further control measures to be carried out. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d]

(2.) The beneficiary *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d] shall *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-1f322603568d392aad3d796d50cf17f8] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d] may *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d] be given the opportunity to sign the report during the check to attest his presence at the check and to add observations. Where *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d] the relevant authority makes[^c-af186bd5f0f9b3fe62ec772455e34d8b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d] use of a control report established by electronic means during the check, the competent authority shall provide for the possibility of an electronic signature by the beneficiary or the control report shall be sent without delay to the beneficiary giving him the opportunity to sign the report and to add any observations. Where any non-compliance is found the beneficiary shall receive a copy of the control report. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d]

(3.) Where *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d] the relevant authority carries[^c-4eff6e5aed2fbb8c9dc2853466b8f814] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d] out on-the-spot checks pursuant to this Regulation in conjunction with inspections pursuant to Regulation (EC) No 1082/2003, the control report shall be supplemented by reports in accordance with Article 2(5) of that Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d]

(4.) Where on-the-spot checks carried out in accordance with this Regulation reveal cases of non-compliance with Title I of Regulation (EC) No 1760/2000 or Regulation (EC) No 21/2004, copies of the control report provided for in this Article shall be sent without delay to the authorities responsible for the implementation of those Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8776762b2442c6ba52f092e70cd91d8d]

### CHAPTER IV — Specific rules

#### Article 44 (affected by repeal, see note). Rules on control results in respect of regional or collective ecological focus areas[^c-114cb09e367186db334143fb0c6eb759]

In the case of regional or collective implementation in accordance with Article 46(5) or (6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, the area of the common contiguous ecological focus areas determined shall be allocated to each participant proportionally to his share in the common ecological areas based on what he has declared pursuant to Article 18 of this Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-114cb09e367186db334143fb0c6eb759]

For the purpose of applying Article 26 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 640/2014 to each participant in a regional or collective implementation, the ecological focus area determined shall be the sum of the allocated share of the common ecological focus areas determined as referred to in the first subparagraph of this Article and the ecological focus areas determined in respect of the individual obligation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-114cb09e367186db334143fb0c6eb759]

#### Article 45. Verification of the tetrahydrocannabinol content in hemp growth (affected by repeal, see note)[^c000012][^c000012]

**No longer in force.**

## TITLE IV — NON-AREA-RELATED AND NON-ANIMAL-RELATED RURAL DEVELOPMENT MEASURES

### CHAPTER I — Introductory provision

#### Article 46. Scope[^M_F_e354fe3e-afc3-4f6c-c128-e7988ee40fed]

This Title shall apply to expenditure incurred under the measures provided for in Articles 14 to 20, Article 21(1) with the exception of the annual premium under points (a) and (b), Article 27, Article 28(9), Articles 35 and 36, Article 39b and Article 51(2) *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9a03f684ae97dd064aab511fa953cb8c] of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013, in Article 35(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and in Article 20, Article 36(a)(vi), (b)(ii), (vi) and (vii), Article 36(b)(i) and (iii) as far as the establishment cost is concerned, and Articles 52 and 63 of Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005.

### CHAPTER II — Checks

#### Section 1 — General provisions

##### Article 47. Applications for support, payment claims and other declarations

(1.) The relevant authority[^c-8f0bfb46efa894131ccd6e2c23e05e87] shall provide for appropriate procedures for submitting applications for support, payment claims and other declarations relating to non-area-related or non-animal-related rural development measures.

(2.) For measures under Articles 15(1)(b), 16(1) and 19(1)(c), *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-41a302358f19ecdd9212e68eb6ef662c] and Article 27 of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013, the beneficiary shall submit an annual payment claim.

#### Section 2 — Provisions for checks

##### Article 48. Administrative checks[^M_E_n66kfl52-z0c6-uofm-fulw-67122a35802da]

*Extent: E+S+N.I.*

(1.) Administrative checks shall be carried out on all applications for support, payment claims or other declarations required to be submitted by a beneficiary or a third party, and shall cover all elements that can be checked and are appropriate to be checked by means of administrative checks. The procedures shall require recording of the control work undertaken, the results of the verification and the measures taken in the event of discrepancies.

(2.) Administrative checks on applications for support shall ensure the compliance of the operation with applicable obligations established by the law applying in the constituent nation[^c-2f2f16afd8511f36de3baea5c88b1e35] or by the rural development programme, including those of public procurement, State aid and other obligatory standards and requirements. The checks shall in particular include verification of:

- ((a)) the eligibility of the beneficiary;
- ((b)) the eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations of the operation for which support is requested;
- ((c)) compliance with the selection criteria, where those criteria apply;[^c000013]
- ((d)) the eligibility of the costs of the operation, including compliance with the category of costs or calculation method to be used when the operation or part of it falls under Article 67(1)(b), (c) and (d) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013;
- ((e)) for costs referred to in Article 67(1)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, excluding contributions in kind and depreciation, a verification of the reasonableness of the costs submitted. The costs shall be evaluated using a suitable evaluation system, such as reference costs, a comparison of different offers or an evaluation committee. For operations with a support rate up to 30 % or for operations supported under Article 35 of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013, the verification of the reasonableness of the costs may be carried out at the stage of the administrative checks on payment claims. For operations with eligible costs up to EUR 5 000, the reasonableness of the costs may be established by a draft budget agreed *ex ante* by the managing authority.

(3.) Administrative checks on payment claims shall include in particular, and where appropriate for the claim in question, verification of:

- ((a)) the completed operation compared with the operation for which the application for support was granted;
- ((b)) the costs incurred and the payments made, except where a form or method as referred to in Article 67(1)(b), (c) or (d) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 is applied.

(4.) Administrative checks shall include procedures to avoid irregular double financing with other schemes in the constituent nations[^c-3aab99ac7e97a7997bc550c6c298a639] and with the previous programming period. Where financing from other sources exists, those checks shall ensure that the total support received does not breach the maximum permissible amounts or support rates.

(5. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-7f1620e8760062f4ed72b11123b67642] Administrative checks on investment operations shall include at least one visit to the operation supported or the investment site to verify the realisation of the investment. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7f1620e8760062f4ed72b11123b67642]

However, the competent authority may decide not to carry out such visits for duly justified reasons, such as the following: *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7f1620e8760062f4ed72b11123b67642]

- ((a)) the operation is included in the sample for an on-the-spot check to be carried out in accordance with Article 49; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7f1620e8760062f4ed72b11123b67642]
- ((b)) the competent authority considers that the operation in question is a small investment; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7f1620e8760062f4ed72b11123b67642]
- ((c)) the competent authority considers that the risk that the conditions for receiving support are not met is low, or that the risk that the investment has not been realised is low. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7f1620e8760062f4ed72b11123b67642]

The decision referred to in the second subparagraph and its justification shall be recorded. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7f1620e8760062f4ed72b11123b67642]

(6.)[^c000014] As regards the financial instruments referred to in Article 38(1)[^c-c27f7688512adc96ccb7d85c81974a63] and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, paragraphs 1 to 5 of this Article shall neither apply to the contribution to the financial instrument nor to the support to the final recipient. However, Articles 58 and 59 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 and Article 9 of Commission Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014[^f00024] shall apply.

##### Article 48. Administrative checks[^M_E_6f9zxcof-w1ql-qmqa-xexw-67122a3580248]

*Extent: W*

(1.) Administrative checks shall be carried out on all applications for support, payment claims or other declarations required to be submitted by a beneficiary or a third party, and shall cover all elements that can be checked and are appropriate to be checked by means of administrative checks. The procedures shall require recording of the control work undertaken, the results of the verification and the measures taken in the event of discrepancies.

(2.) Administrative checks on applications for support shall ensure the compliance of the operation with applicable obligations established by the law applying in the constituent nation[^V_W_656af979-9ec6-8e29-11c4-b3a93e088f5b] or by the rural development programme, including those of public procurement, [^c-1914b365768f8bf9728b7034e1ee501b]... and other obligatory standards and requirements. The checks shall in particular include verification of:

- ((a)) the eligibility of the beneficiary;
- ((b)) the eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations of the operation for which support is requested;
- ((c)) compliance with the selection criteria, where those criteria apply;[^V_W_70324f9a-e243-a5e4-e31d-b83f0d0169f7]
- ((d)) the eligibility of the costs of the operation, including compliance with the category of costs or calculation method to be used when the operation or part of it falls under Article 67(1)(b), (c) and (d) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013;
- ((e)) for costs referred to in Article 67(1)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, excluding contributions in kind and depreciation, a verification of the reasonableness of the costs submitted. The costs shall be evaluated using a suitable evaluation system, such as reference costs, a comparison of different offers or an evaluation committee. For operations with a support rate up to 30 % or for operations supported under Article 35 of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013, the verification of the reasonableness of the costs may be carried out at the stage of the administrative checks on payment claims. For operations with eligible costs up to £5 000[^c-645411bf52db2a8c794f6fc135787881], the reasonableness of the costs may be established by a draft budget agreed [^c-aaf8f34bf61dfc9a8a0627c7795c4b99]... by the managing authority.

(3.) Administrative checks on payment claims shall include in particular, and where appropriate for the claim in question, verification of:

- ((a)) the completed operation compared with the operation for which the application for support was granted;
- ((b)) the costs incurred and the payments made, except where a form or method as referred to in Article 67(1)(b), (c) or (d) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 is applied.

(4.) Administrative checks shall include procedures to avoid irregular double financing with other schemes in the constituent nations[^V_W_66a189a8-388c-b9c4-127a-f13f93c0c562] and with the previous programming period. Where financing from other sources exists, those checks shall ensure that the total support received does not breach the maximum permissible amounts or support rates.

(5.) Administrative checks on investment operations shall include at least one visit to the operation supported or the investment site to verify the realisation of the investment. Those checks shall, to the extent possible, be carried out before the final payment is made for an operation.[^c-29143c6310128e5e748ee43f36f21f50]

However, the competent authority may decide not to carry out such visits for duly justified reasons, such as the following:

- ((a)) [^c-ed46dbdfdb973a3680614b8b92e1520d]...
- ((b)) the competent authority considers that the operation in question is a small investment;
- ((c)) the competent authority considers that the risk that the conditions for receiving support are not met is low, or that the risk that the investment has not been realised is low.

The decision referred to in the second subparagraph and its justification shall be recorded.

(6.)[^V_W_da86c10a-0469-9b57-bae7-d4607dbc4c53] As regards the financial instruments referred to in Article 38(1)[^V_W_e3725dc5-a336-0a8b-b40f-bcc665347d4d] and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, paragraphs 1 to 5 of this Article shall neither apply to the contribution to the financial instrument nor to the support to the final recipient. However, Articles 58 and 59 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 and Article 9 of Commission Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014[^f00024] shall apply.

##### Article 49 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). On-the-spot-checks[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

(1. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] The relevant authority[^c-fcd348819326ee37e776ecb2bf70ac57] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] shall organise on-the-spot checks on approved operations using an appropriate sampling basis. Those checks shall, to the extent possible, be carried out before the final payment is made for an operation. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

By way of derogation from the first sub-paragraph, no physical inspection is required for the on-the-spot checks related to claim year 2021. The competent authority will draw a definitive conclusion from the use of photo-interpretation of satellite or aerial ortho-images or other relevant evidence including such evidence provided by the beneficiary at the request of the competent authority. *(Extent: S)*[^c-ad9fa749a5a72820c07dbfddea7e1146] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

(2. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] The inspectors undertaking the on-the-spot check shall not have been involved in administrative checks of the same operation. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

##### Article 50 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Control rate and sampling of on-the-spot-checks[^M_E_kbfdjoij-srd4-bsp1-6mwd-667307d312339][^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

*Extent: E+W+N.I.*

(1. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] The expenditure covered by on-the-spot checks shall represent at least 5 % of the expenditure referred to in Article 46 and co-financed by *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] rural development support[^c-00aeece1e6472a5c555b508f28e201f7] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] which is claimed to the paying agency each calendar year and which does not concern operations for which only advance payments have been claimed. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

Where an operation subject to on-the-spot check has received advance or interim payments, those payments shall be counted towards the expenditure covered by on-the-spot checks as referred to in the first subparagraph.[^c000015] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

As regards financial instruments, only the payments to the final recipients subject to on-the-spot checks shall be counted towards the expenditure covered by on-the-spot checks as referred to in the first subparagraph. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

(2. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] Only checks carried out until the date foreseen for submission of the control data and control statistics according to Article 9 shall be counted towards the achievement of the minimum level referred to in paragraph 1. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

Payment claims found not to be eligible after administrative checks shall not be counted towards the achievement of the minimum level referred to in paragraph 1. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

(3. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] Only checks meeting all the requirements of Articles 49 and 51 may be counted towards achievement of the minimum level referred to in paragraph 1. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

(4. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] The sample of approved operations to be checked in accordance with paragraph 1 shall take into account in particular: *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

- ((a)) the need to check an appropriate mix of types and sizes of operations; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]
- ((b)) any risk factors identified following *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] [^c-9d74181b6f8e7b1c95d0a37c5e10c068]... checks; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]
- ((c)) the type of operation’s contribution to the risk of error in implementation of the rural development programme; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]
- ((d)) the need to maintain a balance between the measures and types of operations; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]
- ((e)) the need to select randomly between 30 % and 40 % of expenditure. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

(5. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] Where on-the-spot checks reveal any significant non-compliance in the context of a support measure or type of operation, the competent authority shall increase the control rate to an appropriate level in the following calendar year for the measure or type of operation concerned. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

(6. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] By way of derogation from paragraph 1, *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] the relevant authority[^c-2d94364b3fd3bf82d8286b0a48c53ed3] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] may decide to reduce the minimum level of on-the-spot checks carried out each calendar year as referred to in paragraph 1 to 3 % of the amount co-financed by *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] the rural development support[^c-f0d4b8b3d718fbab9895b0cd89fff041] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

The relevant authority[^c-692ef423705f678dd4ff4a5897b7a866] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] may apply the first subparagraph only if the general conditions for reducing the minimum level of on-the-spot checks laid down *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] [^c-66f9b4b0158ca835f3fbb08d0bc8144a]... in accordance with Article 62(2)(b) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 are fulfilled. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

They shall apply the minimum level of on-the-spot checks referred to in paragraph 1 as of the following calendar year. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

##### Article 50. Control rate and sampling of on-the-spot-checks[^M_E_dpwxgqbd-i61m-dtdt-fcy9-667307d3122bc]

*Extent: S*

(1.) The expenditure covered by on-the-spot checks shall represent at least[^V_S_bc36cee3-3026-f0e0-8e28-bb34805c6f03] 3%[^c-558476e6cc8e6d8ce0277c795c3444e6] of the expenditure referred to in Article 46 and co-financed by rural development support[^V_S_fb3a02e8-f6dd-ff80-925e-401ecdecf6ba] which is claimed to the paying agency each calendar year and which does not concern operations for which only advance payments have been claimed.

Where an operation subject to on-the-spot check has received advance or interim payments, those payments shall be counted towards the expenditure covered by on-the-spot checks as referred to in the first subparagraph.[^V_S_89f45f43-420e-f949-9b1a-e971bef39d1f]

As regards financial instruments, only the payments to the final recipients subject to on-the-spot checks shall be counted towards the expenditure covered by on-the-spot checks as referred to in the first subparagraph.[^V_S_06e2a0bb-601f-a1e5-5bd2-fa4cb41bc71e]

(2.) Only checks carried out until the date foreseen for submission of the control data and control statistics according to Article 9 shall be counted towards the achievement of the minimum level referred to in paragraph 1.

Payment claims found not to be eligible after administrative checks shall not be counted towards the achievement of the minimum level referred to in paragraph 1.

(3.) Only checks meeting all the requirements of Articles 49 and 51 may be counted towards achievement of the minimum level referred to in paragraph 1.

(4.) The sample of approved operations to be checked in accordance with paragraph 1 shall take into account in particular:

- ((a)) the need to check an appropriate mix of types and sizes of operations;
- ((b)) any risk factors identified following [^V_S_fee55f94-36e3-062a-2c4a-093f31eca1fb]... checks;
- ((c)) the type of operation’s contribution to the risk of error in implementation of the rural development programme;
- ((d)) the need to maintain a balance between the measures and types of operations;
- ((e)) the need to select randomly between 30 % and 40 % of expenditure.

(5.)[^c-aa850c311df3e4542434647afced429d] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

##### Article 51 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Content of the on-the-spot-checks[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

(1. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] On-the-spot checks shall verify that the operation has been implemented in accordance with the applicable rules and shall cover all the eligibility criteria, commitments and other obligations relating to the conditions for the granting of support, which can be checked at the time of the visit and have not been the subject of administrative checks. They shall ensure that the operation is eligible for *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] rural development[^c-5ba6f21c4ebd97612b7b336080b22e47] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] support. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

(2. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] On-the-spot checks shall verify the accuracy of the data declared by the beneficiary against underlying documents. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

This includes a verification that the payment claims submitted by the beneficiary are supported by accounting or other documents, including, where necessary, a check on the accuracy of the data in the payment claim on the basis of data or commercial documents held by third parties. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

(3. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] On-the-spot checks shall verify that the use or intended use of the operation is consistent with the use described in the application for support and for which the support was granted. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

(4. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] Except in exceptional circumstances, duly recorded and explained by the competent authorities, on-the-spot checks shall include a visit to the place where the operation is implemented or, if the operation is intangible, to the operation promoter. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

(5. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] As regards the financial instruments referred to in *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] Article 38(1)[^c-e27b370bfd8498c64b392dc0042f5d5d] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c] and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, paragraphs 1 to 4 of this Article shall neither apply to the contribution to the financial instrument nor to the support to the final recipient. However, Articles 58 and 59 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 and Article 9 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014 shall apply. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7f0fa0ad55b33b085914b4e159e003c]

##### Article 52. Ex-post checks[^M_E_8b18z841-qspn-aqsm-3fnu-6717c4c5d2902]

*Extent: W*

(1.) Ex-post checks shall be carried out on investment operations to verify the respect of commitments pursuant to Article 71 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 or detailed in the rural development programme.

(2.) The *ex-post* checks shall cover in each calendar year at least 1 % of rural development support[^V_W_ae49dac5-0337-bdb1-b11d-813c10636b76] expenditure for investment operations that are still subject to commitment as referred to in paragraph 1 and for which the final payment has been made from the rural development support. Only checks carried out until the end of the calendar year in question shall be taken into account.

(3.) The sample for operations to be checked in accordance with paragraph 1 shall be based on an analysis of the risks and financial impact of different operations, types of operations or measures. A sample shall be selected randomly.[^c-adb0296858a350d42db9bda4e6171347]

##### Article 52. Ex-post checks[^M_E_nask6cun-crrb-0mvg-zfha-6717c4c5d2a09]

*Extent: S*

(1.) Ex-post checks shall be carried out on investment operations to verify the respect of commitments pursuant to Article 71 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 or detailed in the rural development programme.

(2.) The *ex-post* checks shall cover in each calendar year at least 1 % of rural development support[^V_S_8a5f8a0d-8d06-9036-a7f5-ed450b9971fb] expenditure for investment operations that are still subject to commitment as referred to in paragraph 1 and for which the final payment has been made from the rural development support. Only checks carried out until the end of the calendar year in question shall be taken into account.

By way of derogation from the first sub-paragraph, the control rate for ex-post checks shall be at least 0.6[^c-3a900ebdd00a178723b593121f5b1746] % in calendar year 2021. In calendar year 2021, no physical inspection is required for ex-post checks. The competent authority will draw a definitive conclusion from the use of photo-interpretation of satellite or aerial ortho-images or other relevant evidence including such evidence provided by the beneficiary at the request of the competent authority.

(3.) The sample for operations to be checked in accordance with paragraph 1 shall be based on an analysis of the risks and financial impact of different operations, types of operations or measures. Between 20 % and 25 % of the sample shall be selected randomly.

##### Article 52. Ex-post checks[^M_E_zfhp86va-7s4i-tr28-j1ex-6717cf42f22ec]

*Extent: N.I.*

(1.) Ex-post checks shall be carried out on investment operations to verify the respect of commitments pursuant to Article 71 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 or detailed in the rural development programme.

(2.) The *ex-post* checks shall cover in each calendar year at least 1 % of rural development support[^V_NI_e501e01d-1974-48ee-62dc-f4367194aa5d] expenditure for investment operations that are still subject to commitment as referred to in paragraph 1 and for which the final payment has been made from the rural development support. Only checks carried out until the end of the calendar year in question shall be taken into account.

(3.) The sample for operations to be checked in accordance with paragraph 1 shall be based on an analysis of the risks and financial impact of different operations, types of operations or measures. Between 20 % and 25 % of the sample shall be selected randomly.

##### Article 53. Control report

(1.) Every on-the-spot check under this Section shall be the subject of a control report which makes it possible to review the details of the checks carried out. The report shall indicate in particular:

- ((a)) the measures and applications or payment claims checked;
- ((b)) the persons present;
- ((c)) whether notice was given to the beneficiary of the visit and, if so, the period of prior notice;
- ((d)) the results of the checks and, where applicable, any particular observations;
- ((e)) any further control measures to be carried out.

(2.) Paragraph 1 shall apply *mutatis mutandis* to *ex-post* checks under this Section.

(3.) The beneficiary shall be given the opportunity to sign the report during the check, to attest the beneficiary’s presence at the check and to add observations. Where the relevant authority makes[^c-1d6c806c703a1af923a2812eced0f344] use of a control report established by electronic means during the check, the competent authority shall provide for the possibility of an electronic signature by the beneficiary or the control report shall be sent without delay to the beneficiary giving him the opportunity to sign the report and to add any observations. Where any non-compliance is found, the beneficiary shall receive a copy of the control report.
